title = "Which of the five safety readouts is still unclaimed"

layman_summary = ("A literature check on five candidate ways to score a language model's safety from its internal "
 "activations. One candidate is dead: a peer-reviewed ACL 2026 paper already publishes the same measurement. "
 "Two survive with a real but narrow gap, one survives only at the activation level, and one is thin. The most "
 "useful discovery was an accident: two separate papers show that a model's ability to RECOGNISE harm barely "
 "changes when its refusal behaviour is surgically removed, so half the planned comparison is predicted to show "
 "nothing unless the measurement targets the refusal machinery instead.")

summary = ("Web-only saturation and anchor-verification pass, 2026-09-20, ~$0 spend, no cuts taken. All 26 core arXiv "
 "ids resolved (0 unresolved, 0 mis-cited) plus 20 newly discovered ids. VERDICTS: K1 arming interaction = PARTIALLY "
 "SCOOPED (HARC 2607.00572 owns the response-site readout via Eq 2 but never crosses the two factors, uses no matched "
 "twins, and reports no interaction); K2 prior + evidence slope = OPEN at the activation level but its construct is "
 "published behaviourally by IRT 2608.05086 over 192 models; K3 benign-only footprint = PARTIALLY SCOOPED, with "
 "LatentBiopsy 2603.27412 - not Skin-Deep - as the true nearest relative; K4 persistence time constant = CLOSED by "
 "N-GLARE's JR Min/Max (ACL 2026 Long 1334, Eq 9), a per-model persistence scalar over 40+ models; K5 per-domain "
 "profile = PARTIALLY SCOOPED and thin. FOUR deliverable-level competitors, not two: N-GLARE, Skin-Deep/GFS, the "
 "273-checkpoint abliteration audit 2607.01854, and IRT-10-items. F1 ANSWERED DECISIVELY: HARC does print numeric "
 "cross-position cosines, for Qwen, at 0.19/0.10 (L12) and 0.31/0.30 (L27), so lane B's |cos| <= 0.50 gate is expected "
 "to PASS; 2604.18901's independent 73+/-7 degree protocol angle (cos ~ 0.29) converges on the same answer. F2 "
 "OVERTURNS THE PLANNING RECON: 2604.18901 prints +/-0.003 twice and 73 degrees twice, for four different quantities, "
 "and the hypothesis's readings were the right ones. Three new adverse priors the run had not seen: HRCI_repr "
 "(2606.16349) is a parent-free single-checkpoint coupling index whose authors report it is NOT a safety score; the "
 "Entanglement Wall gets AUROC 0.590-0.690 on XSTest-style twins; and harm recognition is invariant to abliteration in "
 "two independent papers. Ten baselines specified for reimplementation, including the newly recovered HRCI_repr formula.")

answer = """FIVE VERDICTS, one line each (uniform rule: CLOSED = same quantity, >1 model, presented as a cross-model score).

K1 ARMING INTERACTION - PARTIALLY SCOOPED (high confidence). HARC 2607.00572 owns the response-site readout: its Eq 2 fits v_harm^resp by difference-in-means mean-pooled over the first 32 response tokens, and our early window (continuation tokens 5-20) sits INSIDE that pool, so the instrument is not ours to claim. But HARC never crosses request harmfulness with already-written response content, reports no interaction term, has no benign-request/hazardous-prefix cell, and uses a separate safe prompt set D_help rather than matched twins (its prompt-side directions come from 300 AdvBench harmful + 300 UltraChat harmless). The CROSSING and the INTERACTION TERM survive; the response-site fit does not. Must-beat baselines: HARC's own main effect with the request factor discarded; prompt-side diff-in-means at the last request token; first-token refusal logit gap.

K2 PRIOR + EVIDENCE SLOPE - OPEN at the activation level, CONSTRUCT PUBLISHED behaviourally (medium confidence, 6 queries incl. 2 adversarial and 2 recency, one returning nothing). IRT for AI Safety 2608.05086 fits refusal strictness / truthfulness / contextual harm over 192 models and 8 benchmarks - an IRT difficulty/discrimination pair IS a prior/slope pair by another name - and shows ten adaptive items recover a benchmark at 97-99% cost saving. No activation-level relative found. The novel half is the CONTENTLESS forward pass; the slope alone is not novel.

K3 BENIGN-ONLY / WEIGHT-ONLY FOOTPRINT - PARTIALLY SCOOPED, margin narrower than the hypothesis assumes (high confidence). The nearest relative is NOT Skin-Deep, it is LatentBiopsy 2603.27412: 200 safe prompts only, no harmful examples, no parent model, evaluated on Qwen base/instruct/abliterated triplets. Skin-Deep/GFS is both parent-requiring (M and its base counterpart M0) and harmful-prompt-requiring (D_safe), and predicts post-LoRA fragility not current safety. 2607.01854 is reference-anchored. CANARY needs two checkpoints. 2609.20129 is per-input and needs a per-model SAE. Nobody is simultaneously PARENT-FREE, HARMFUL-TEXT-FREE and PER-CHECKPOINT: that intersection is the margin, and it must be stated in exactly those three words.

K4 PERSISTENCE / DECAY TIME CONSTANT - CLOSED. KILL IT. N-GLARE (arXiv 2511.14195, published ACL 2026 Long 1334) Eq 9 defines JR Min/Max = min_s JSS_s(J,R) / max_s JSS_s(J,R) over an arc-length standardised progress axis s in [0,1], described as measuring whether separability "remains stable across the entire trajectory" versus "late-stage collapse of refusal" - a per-model persistence scalar of a safety readout, computed over 40+ models and presented as a cross-model safety score. Fitting an exponential instead of taking a min/max ratio is a parameterisation, not a construct. Iteration 2 must not spend depth here. (HARC Appendix A.3, checked, is a cross-LAYER profile, not a token-position curve, so it adds nothing here either.)

K5 PER-HARM-DOMAIN PROFILE - PARTIALLY SCOOPED, thin (medium confidence). 2609.19366 already has per-category harm representations with category residuals orthogonal to general harm across 11 categories and 3 models; 2507.21141 has 55 subconcept directions spanning a low-rank harmfulness subspace. Behaviourally the construct is doubly published (HarmProfile 2608.14577 explicitly calls its per-category distribution "a model-level risk profile" over 23 LLMs; ACL 2026 Findings 489 reports defense rates swinging 42% within one model by target group). What remains: the DISPERSION STATISTIC and PROFILE FLATTENING as a per-checkpoint signature. A4/2609.14759 is NOT K5's nearest relative - its exceptions are per-model-family, not per-harm-domain.

THE KILL LIST. K4 outright. Plus three things dead as CLAIMS though their lanes survive: (a) the response-site diff-in-means readout is HARC Eq 2, not ours; (b) "a cheap latent safety score exists" is peer-reviewed at 40+ models and <1% cost, so the contribution must be WHICH internal quantity carries the signal and why; (c) "harm recognition survives abliteration" is published twice and any rediscovery is a replication.

F1 - THE COSINE GATE. ANSWERED, AND THE GATE IS EXPECTED TO PASS. The recon recorded "no printed numeric cosine". Overturned: HARC's Appendix A prints same-concept CROSS-POSITION cosines on Qwen-2.5-7B of 0.19 (harm) and 0.10 (ref) at L12, and 0.31 and 0.30 at L27 - all below lane B's |cos| <= 0.50 gate. Within-position response-side coupling is 0.42 (L12) / 0.69 (L27) on Qwen versus 0.45 / 0.27 on Llama. Llama's cross-position values remain heat-map-only. Independent corroboration: 2604.18901 reports 73 +/- 7 degrees between max-pool-over-content and last-token-post-instruction harm directions in the same layer, i.e. cos ~ 0.29. Two papers, two protocols, converging on 0.19-0.31. LANE B PROCEEDS ON THE MAIN BRANCH; the annihilation-residue branch stays pre-committed but is now the unexpected path. Caveats: Qwen-2.5-7B is not Qwen3-4B; HARC's v_harm is AdvBench/UltraChat-fitted, not abliteration-derived; measuring this on Qwen3-4B with r_ablit as one axis is still a first, so report it as a result, just not a surprising one.

F2 - THE 2604.18901 MISATTRIBUTIONS. THE PLANNING RECON WAS WRONG, TWICE; THE HYPOTHESIS WAS RIGHT, TWICE. The paper prints +/-0.003 TWICE: (a) "Abliterated variants ... match their instruction-tuned counterparts within +/-0.003 AUROC" (each with its own direction - the hypothesis's reading) and (b) "instruct-to-abliterated transfer is substantially cleaner than base-to-instruct (angles 11 to 42 degrees, AUROC degradation <= 0.003)" (the recon's reading). It also prints 73 degrees TWICE: (a) the max-pool-vs-last-token PROTOCOL angle of 73 +/- 7 degrees at the same layer (the hypothesis's reading) and (b) Gemma-3's base-to-instruct alignment rotation of 73 degrees with AUROC -0.057 and TPR 0.751 to 0.175 (the recon's reading). Both pairs are verbatim. Any future citation must say WHICH.

F2 HANDOFF TO LANE B - NO INTERIOR MATCHED-O-DAMAGE POINT SHOULD BE EXPECTED. Instruct-to-abliterated angles are 11-42 degrees (cos 0.74-0.98) with AUROC degradation <= 0.003, and the abliterated model's own-direction AUROC matches instruct within +/-0.003. The edit leaves the request axis nearly intact. Report the criterion UNMET rather than relaxing it. Also note our abliterated checkpoint is mlabonne/, not the huihui-ai/ line the +/-0.003 was measured on.

F3 - N-GLARE. JSS(A,B) = (1/|G|) sum_G (1/I) sum_i JS_i^G(A,B) over Jensen-Shannon divergences of geometric-turning-angle distributions per progress slice, aggregated across slices and layer groups (Eq 7); proxies JB/PB Ratio (Eq 8) and JR Min/Max (Eq 9). INPUT REQUIREMENT: four dialogue families - Baseline, PlainQuery, Jailbreak, Ideal Refusal - so it is NOT a 0-prompt or few-prompt method, and our prompt-budget curve must be read against that, not against a benchmark. NO NUMERIC KENDALL'S TAU IS PRINTED IN THE PUBLISHED RUNNING TEXT (only "tau remains consistently high, with p-values far below conventional significance thresholds"; Figures 4-5 and Appendix Tables 7-8 hold the values) - do not write "N-GLARE achieves tau = X". NO PUBLIC CODE (full-text grep for code/github/release returns nothing), so JSS is NOT an implementable baseline in iteration 1; lane C cites it and states the comparison it could not run.

F4 - PREFILL PROTOCOL (2607.14147). Confirmed and explained: "The dramatic full-residual 'restoration' is generic disruption, since a benign-patch control also hits 100%." Lane B's demotion of full-residual patching to a labelled positive control is correct. The load-bearing rungs are the dose-matched positional control (first half 42%, whole response 41%, second half 6%, onset 9%; Qwen2.5-1.5B, L12, n=100) and the refuse-state transfer (74% held-out vs 0% random). Its pre-registered 2x2 crosses harm with SURFACE trigger words, not with response content, so it does not scoop K1.

F5 - ANTI-RANKING (2608.09624). Verbatim: "Among wrapped harmful prompts the outcome AUROC is 0.220, which places the attacks that succeeded below the attacks that failed." Reproduced across three target models, seven attack families and two judges. The paper must engage it.

DELIVERABLE-LEVEL COMPETITORS - FOUR, NOT TWO. (1) N-GLARE, ACL 2026, latent-only, 40+ models, <1% cost, peer-reviewed. (2) Skin-Deep/GFS, single scalar, 21 models, but parent- and harmful-prompt-requiring and predicts future fragility. (3) 2607.01854, 273-checkpoint registry, activation refusal-gap + weight-recovery energy, AUROC 0.95 - but reference-anchored. (4) IRT 2608.05086, ten adaptive items, 192 models - text-only, therefore a baseline, but it owns the "few prompts" half of the ask. Also mandatory to cite: 2606.08044 (the audit gap - the best motivation for an activation metric), 2502.10487 (black-box fast proxies predict ASR at Spearman 0.94 - the ceiling), 2605.20241 (layer-wise margin profiles).

THE SINGLE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL FINDING, WHICH IS NOT A NOVELTY VERDICT. Two independent papers report that prompt-side HARM-RECOGNITION geometry is essentially INVARIANT to abliteration: +/-0.003 AUROC across 12 models (2604.18901) and <= 0.015 AUROC on Qwen base/instruct/abliterated triplets (2603.27412). Two of our four panel checkpoints are Qwen3-4B and mlabonne/Qwen3-4B-abliterated. Any candidate whose readout is fundamentally "can this checkpoint recognise harm" has a PUBLISHED PRIOR OF ~ZERO EFFECT on that pair. Candidates must read the refusal/gate side or the arming interaction. Put this in every lane's power calculation.

THE SECOND MOST CONSEQUENTIAL, ALSO NEW. 2606.16349 defines HRCI_repr (Eq 9) = 0.5*|h^T r| + 0.5*(mean squared canonical correlation between the harmfulness and refusal subspaces) - a PARENT-FREE, SINGLE-CHECKPOINT, interaction-flavoured activation scalar - tracks it over 100 checkpoints per regime against XSTest refusal, ASR, StrongREJECT and utility, and concludes: "Thus low coupling is not a safety score." That is the closest published object to this run's own ambition, and it is a scaled negative result. It is now mandatory baseline G10, it is implementable in a few lines, and the paper must answer it rather than cite it in passing. Numbers: HRCI_repr 0.0784 (step 50) to 0.0205 (step 500), a 73.9% drop, with fixed ASR 0 to 0.2500 and XSTest refusal 1.0000 to 0.2280; SFT control moves only 0.0217 to 0.0190 while staying high-ASR.

A THIRD ADVERSE PRIOR LANE A MUST PRICE IN. The Entanglement Wall 2607.13075 measures activation probes on XSTest-style matched twin cohorts - exactly lane A's item type - and gets AUROC 0.590-0.690 on the full Twin-n163 cohort with no pair-boundary fitting, while false-blocking 79.6-100% of XSTest at 95% in-corpus TPR. If lane A's twin numbers land above that band, check for leakage before celebrating.

BASELINE SPECS (full text in research_report.md section 7): G1 card/repo regex in BOTH term-swept and name-free variants (0.953 vs 0.642 in this program - report both or the baseline is inflated by the label it predicts); G2 black-box greedy refusal rate with the named safe-completion-opener failure mode; G3 first-token refusal logit gap (LOGIT-ONLY, a baseline by invariant); G4 Fisher-ratio cluster separation over the [0.5L, 0.85L] band; G5 held-out diff-in-means with the mandatory d>>n in-sample AUROC-1.000 warning and TPR@1%FPR reported alongside AUROC; G6 raw hidden vectors; G7 JSS NOT IMPLEMENTABLE; G8 GFS / 2607.01854 z-sum / CANARY all PARENT-REQUIRING and must sit in a separate column; G9 the r_s = 0.94 black-box proxy ceiling; G10 HRCI_repr, fully specified and parent-free, the single most important baseline in the block.

ID RESOLUTION: 26/26 core ids resolved, 0 unresolved, 0 mis-cited, plus 20 new ids discovered and resolved. The only unresolvable citation is the companion methods note "Calibrating Interpretability Instruments Before Trusting Their Verdicts" (named in 2609.14759's comments field, no standalone arXiv id found); no verdict here rests on it. Note that 2604.09544 IS a real paper but is about parameter-level pruning of harmful compliance, not the harmfulness/refusal split - the right citation for that split is 2507.11878 (NeurIPS 2025)."""

follow_ups = [

Closes by: a TABLE-AWARE parse (not a regex grep) of Appendix Table 7 and Table 8 of https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.1334.pdf. Closes by: reading Section 3.1-3.2 of arxiv.org/html/2606.16349 for the carrier-extraction and subspace-dimension protocol.",

If it does, those are key-joinable against K1's 2x2 and would either sharpen K1's margin or narrow it further. Closes by: fetching arxiv.org/html/2608.30585 and grepping for the per-condition contrast tables.",

No published number exists for this pair (F1 gives Qwen-2.5-7B and a protocol angle, not our checkpoint), so lane B's measured value would be the first of its kind and should be reported as a primary result, with 0.50 treated as a pre-registered branch point rather than a validity claim."]

out = dict(title=title, layman_summary=layman_summary, summary=summary, answer=answer,

Run `run_YqmEFECOIR3D` iter_1 **GEN_ART / gen_art_research_1** (2026-09-20), executing
[[project-qwen3-safety-genplan-research-iter1-run-yqmefecoir3d]]. Web-only, ~$0, no cuts taken.
Outputs: `research_report.md` (58 KB, 10 sections) + `research_out.json` == `.terminal_claude_agent_struct_out.json`
(32 sources, 59 passages, **all 59 self-verified by an independent live re-fetch before writing**).

## VERDICTS
K1 arming interaction **PARTIALLY SCOOPED** · K2 prior+slope **OPEN at activation level** (construct published
behaviourally by IRT 2608.05086, 192 models) · K3 benign-only **PARTIALLY SCOOPED** · **K4 persistence CLOSED**
· K5 per-domain **PARTIALLY SCOOPED (thin)**.

## THE FIVE THINGS WORTH REMEMBERING

1. **THE PLANNING RECON'S TWO "CORRECTIONS" ARE BOTH WRONG — because 2604.18901 prints each number TWICE.**
   ±0.003 appears as (a) abliterated-vs-instruct **own-direction AUROC match** (the hypothesis's reading) AND
   (b) instruct→abliterated **transfer** degradation at 11°–42° (the recon's reading). 73° appears as
   (a) **max-pool-over-content vs last-token-post-instruction protocol angle, 73°±7°, same layer** (the
   hypothesis's reading) AND (b) **Gemma-3's base→instruct rotation**, ΔAUROC −0.057, TPR 0.751→0.175 (the
   recon's). **How to apply: a snippet that "contradicts" a cited number may be a DIFFERENT sentence carrying
   the same digits. Grep the whole paper for the number before declaring a misattribution.**

2. **F1 ANSWERED, OPPOSITE TO THE FEAR. HARC DOES print numeric cross-position cosines — in Appendix A, for
   Qwen-2.5-7B: 0.19 (harm) / 0.10 (ref) at L12, 0.31 / 0.30 at L27.** All below the |cos| ≤ 0.50 gate ⇒
   **lane B proceeds on the MAIN branch**, annihilation-residue is now the unexpected path. Independent
   corroboration: 2604.18901's 73°±7° protocol angle ⇒ cos ≈ 0.29. Within-position response coupling:
   Qwen 0.42 (L12) / 0.69 (L27) vs **Llama 0.45 / 0.27**. Llama's cross-position values are heat-map-only.
   **A plain page fetch TRUNCATES arXiv HTML around ~50 KB and drops the appendices — the recon's "no printed
   cosine" was a fetch artifact. Always escalate to full-document regex grep before concluding NOT-PRINTED.**

3. **THE PANEL'S instruct↔abliterated CONTRAST IS PREDICTED NULL for any harm-recognition readout.** Two
   independent papers: 2604.18901 **±0.003 AUROC** (12 models) and 2603.27412 LatentBiopsy **≤0.015 AUROC** on
   **Qwen base/instruct/abliterated triplets**. 2 of our 4 checkpoints are exactly that pair. ⇒ the metric must
   read the **refusal/gate side or the arming interaction**, never harm recognition. Put it in every power calc.

4. **K4 IS DEAD: N-GLARE Eq 9 already IS the persistence statistic.** `JR Min/Max = min_s JSS_s(J,R) / max_s JSS_s(J,R)`
   over an **arc-length standardised progress axis s∈[0,1]**, one scalar per model, 40+ models, ACL 2026 Long 1334.
   JSS itself (Eq 7) = JSD of geometric-turning-angle distributions averaged over slices × layer groups.
   **Needs FOUR dialogue families (Baseline / PlainQuery / Jailbreak / Ideal Refusal) ⇒ NOT a 0-or-few-prompt
   method. NO numeric Kendall's τ in the published running text** (only "τ remains consistently high"; values
   live in Figs 4–5 / App. Tables 7–8) — never write "N-GLARE achieves τ = X". **No public code** (confirmed
   independently of the sibling artifact). HARC's Appendix A.3 is a cross-LAYER profile, not a token curve.

5. **THE RUN'S AMBITION HAS A NAMED, SCALED, PUBLISHED NEGATIVE RESULT: HRCI_repr (2606.16349 Eq 9).**
   `HRCI_repr = ½·|hᵀr| + ½·(mean squared canonical correlation between the harmfulness and refusal subspaces)`
   — **parent-free, single-checkpoint, interaction-flavoured**, tracked over 100 checkpoints per regime, and the
   authors' own verdict is *"Thus low coupling is not a safety score."* (0.0784 step-50 → 0.0205 step-500, −73.9%,
   ASR 0→0.25, XSTest refusal 1.00→0.228; SFT control 0.0217→0.0190 and stays high-ASR.) It is now **baseline G10**
   and is implementable in a few lines. Any candidate that does not beat it has no story.

## OTHER DESIGN-CHANGING FACTS
- **K3's nearest relative is NOT Skin-Deep — it is 2603.27412 LatentBiopsy**: 200 SAFE prompts only, no harmful
  examples, no parent, on Qwen triplets. K3's surviving margin is exactly **PARENT-FREE ∧ HARMFUL-TEXT-FREE ∧
  PER-CHECKPOINT** (LatentBiopsy is per-PROMPT). Skin-Deep needs M0 **and** D_safe (both quoted). 2607.01854
  (273-checkpoint registry, AUROC 0.95) is reference-anchored. CANARY needs two checkpoints. 2609.20129 needs a
  per-model SAE and is per-input.
- **FOUR deliverable-level competitors, not two**: + 2607.01854 and **IRT 2608.05086 (ten adaptive items, 97–99%
  cheaper, 192 models)** — the latter owns the "few prompts" half of the commissioned ask, text-only.
- **Lane A's twin design has a published ceiling**: 2607.13075 Entanglement Wall gets **AUROC 0.590–0.690 on the
  full Twin-n163** XSTest-style cohort with no pair-boundary fitting, and false-blocks 79.6–100% of XSTest at 95%
  in-corpus TPR. Above that band ⇒ check for leakage first.
- **Realistic effect size for a cheap activation metric vs behaviour: r = −0.546 (AMS 2608.05578, 14 configs).**
  Black-box ceiling to beat: **Spearman 0.94** (2502.10487).
- HARC uses **no matched twins** (300 AdvBench / 300 UltraChat prompt-side; D_help for benign continuations) and
  **no crossing** — K1's margin is the crossing + the interaction term, not the response-site readout (Eq 2).
- 2607.14147: full-residual "restoration" hits 100% **but so does a benign-patch control** ⇒ generic disruption,
  lane B's demotion is right. Its pre-registered 2×2 is **harm × SURFACE**, not harm × prefix.
- 2604.09544 is a REAL paper but is parameter-pruning; the harm/refusal-split citation is **2507.11878** (NeurIPS 2025).
- All 26 core ids resolved, 0 unresolved; 20 new ids found. Only unresolvable: the 2609.14759 companion note
  *"Calibrating Interpretability Instruments Before Trusting Their Verdicts"* (no standalone arXiv id).

**How to apply:** the throughput trick that made this fit the budget was **backgrounded `&` batches of the
aii-web-tools fetch/grep script (26 arXiv abs pages in one wait, then 5–8 greps per wait)** — serial fetching
would have burned the whole window. And **self-verify every quoted passage by re-fetching its own URL before
writing the JSON**: 2 of 57 quotes failed (LaTeX `1\%` on an abs page; an appendix beyond the fetch truncation)
and were swapped for verifiable ones, taking the artifact from 2 future `status=error` rows to 0.

Related: [[project-qwen3-safety-genplan-research-iter1-run-yqmefecoir3d]],

**Verdicts:** K1 PARTIALLY SCOOPED · K2 OPEN at the activation level (construct published behaviourally) · K3 PARTIALLY SCOOPED · **K4 CLOSED** · K5 PARTIALLY SCOOPED (thin).

**The five results that change the design:**

1. **F1 is answered, and the opposite way to the fear.** HARC *does* print numeric cross-position cosines — Appendix A, Qwen-2.5-7B: **0.19/0.10 at L12, 0.31/0.30 at L27**, all below lane B's |cos| ≤ 0.50 gate. 2604.18901's independent 73°±7° protocol angle (cos ≈ 0.29) converges. **Lane B proceeds on the main branch.** The recon's "no printed cosine" was a fetch-truncation artifact.

2. **Both of the planning recon's "corrections" to 2604.18901 are wrong** — the paper prints ±0.003 twice and 73° twice, for four different quantities, and the hypothesis's readings were the right ones each time.

3. **K4 is dead.** N-GLARE's `JR Min/Max` (ACL 2026 Long 1334, Eq 9) is already a per-model persistence scalar of a safety readout over a standardised progress axis, on 40+ models. Fitting an exponential instead is a parameterisation, not a construct.

4. **The panel's instruct↔abliterated contrast is predicted null for any harm-recognition readout** — ±0.003 (2604.18901) and ≤0.015 on Qwen triplets (2603.27412, which is also K3's true nearest relative, not Skin-Deep). That is 2 of 4 checkpoints.

5. **The run's own ambition has a published negative result**: HRCI_repr (2606.16349 Eq 9) is a parent-free single-checkpoint interaction-like activation index, tracked over 100 checkpoints per regime, whose authors conclude *"low coupling is not a safety score."* It is now mandatory baseline G10 and is implementable in a few lines.

Four deliverable-level competitors exist, not two — N-GLARE (no public code, needs four prompt families, **no numeric τ in the published text**), Skin-Deep/GFS (parent- *and* harmful-prompt-requiring), the 273-checkpoint audit 2607.01854, and IRT-10-items. Ten baselines are specified precisely enough for the three parallel lanes to implement identically. Three follow-ups remain genuinely open, each with the specific fetch that would close it.

K1 ARMING INTERACTION - PARTIALLY SCOOPED (high confidence). HARC [1] owns the response-site readout: its Eq 2 fits the response-position harmfulness direction by difference-in-means mean-pooled over the first 32 response tokens [2], and our early window (continuation tokens 5-20) sits INSIDE that pool, so the instrument is not ours to claim. But HARC never crosses request harmfulness with already-written response content, reports no interaction term, has no benign-request/hazardous-prefix cell, and uses a separate safe prompt set rather than matched twins (its prompt-side directions come from 300 AdvBench harmful plus 300 UltraChat harmless) [2]. The CROSSING and the INTERACTION TERM survive; the response-site fit does not. The adjacent designs confirm the gap: the prefill study's pre-registered 2x2 crosses harm with SURFACE trigger words, not with response content [15]; the roleplay Safety Relay analysis crosses matched harmful/benign requests with a PROMPT WRAPPER, not with the model's own output [28]; and the harm/refusal split that motivates the decomposition is prompt-side [27]. Must-beat baselines: HARC's own main effect with the request factor discarded [1]; prompt-side diff-in-means at the last request token, which is adverse-flagged by final-token probe failures [29]; and the first-token refusal logit gap.

K2 PRIOR PLUS EVIDENCE SLOPE - OPEN at the activation level, CONSTRUCT PUBLISHED behaviourally (medium confidence, 6 queries including 2 adversarial and 2 recency, one returning nothing). Item Response Theory for AI Safety [14] fits refusal strictness, truthfulness and contextual harm over 192 models and 8 safety benchmarks - an IRT difficulty/discrimination pair IS a prior/slope pair by another name - and shows roughly ten adaptive items recover a benchmark at 97-99 percent cost saving [14]. No activation-level relative was found across six queries. The novel half is the CONTENTLESS forward pass; the slope alone is not novel.

K3 BENIGN-ONLY / WEIGHT-ONLY FOOTPRINT - PARTIALLY SCOOPED, margin narrower than the hypothesis assumes (high confidence). The nearest relative is NOT Skin-Deep, it is LatentBiopsy [9]: 200 safe prompts only, no harmful examples required for training, no parent model, and evaluated on Qwen base/instruct/abliterated triplets [9][10]. Skin-Deep/GFS [7] is both parent-requiring and harmful-prompt-requiring - it compares an aligned model against its base counterpart on harmful-request and benign sets [8] - and it predicts post-LoRA refusal retention rather than current safety [7]. The 273-checkpoint audit [13] is reference-anchored and base-to-candidate on both of its signals. CANARY works from a hidden-state difference over two forward passes, so it needs a second checkpoint [24]. Local Sparsity is safe-data-only but is a per-input anomaly detector in a per-model SAE feature space [25]. AMS scores activation geometry across 14 configurations but needs harmful-content concepts [17]. Nobody is simultaneously PARENT-FREE, HARMFUL-TEXT-FREE and PER-CHECKPOINT: that intersection is the margin, and it must be stated in exactly those three words.

K4 PERSISTENCE / DECAY TIME CONSTANT - CLOSED. KILL IT. N-GLARE [4], published at ACL 2026 as Long paper 1334 [3], defines JSS as the Jensen-Shannon divergence of geometric-turning-angle distributions aggregated across progress slices and layer groups (Eq 7) [3], and then Eq 9 defines JR Min/Max as the ratio of the minimum to the maximum slice-wise JSS along an arc-length standardised progress axis running from 0 to 1, explicitly measuring whether separability remains stable across the entire trajectory versus a late-stage collapse of refusal [3]. That is a per-model persistence scalar of a safety readout, computed over more than 40 models and presented as a cross-model safety score [3][4]. Fitting an exponential instead of taking a min/max ratio is a parameterisation, not a construct. Iteration 2 must not spend depth here. The two other trajectory papers do NOT close it and do not rescue it either: SALO finds a layer-token refusal trajectory but builds a per-input jailbreak detector [26], and HARC's Appendix A.3, checked directly, is a cross-LAYER projection profile rather than a token-position curve [2].

K5 PER-HARM-DOMAIN PROFILE - PARTIALLY SCOOPED, thin (medium confidence). Per-category harmfulness representations with category residuals orthogonal to general harm already exist across 11 risk categories and 3 instruction-tuned models [19]. Behaviourally the construct is doubly published: HarmProfile explicitly defines a per-category harmful-output distribution as a model-level risk profile over 23 frontier LLMs [20], and the ACL 2026 Findings audit reports defense rates swinging up to 42 percent within a single model depending only on the target group [21]. What remains unclaimed is the DISPERSION STATISTIC over the profile and PROFILE FLATTENING as a per-checkpoint signature of safety training. The recon's nomination of the harm-keyed-routing paper as K5's nearest relative is OVERTURNED: its exceptions are per-model-family, not per-harm-domain [18].

THE KILL LIST. K4 outright. Plus three things dead as CLAIMS though their lanes survive: (a) the response-site diff-in-means readout is HARC Eq 2, not ours [2]; (b) "a cheap latent safety score exists" is peer-reviewed at 40-plus models and under 1 percent of red-teaming cost [3][4], so the contribution must be WHICH internal quantity carries the signal and why; (c) "harm recognition survives abliteration" is published twice [5][10] and any rediscovery is a replication.

F1 - THE COSINE GATE. ANSWERED, AND THE GATE IS EXPECTED TO PASS. The planning recon recorded "no printed numeric cosine". That is overturned. HARC's Appendix A prints same-concept CROSS-POSITION cosines on Qwen-2.5-7B of 0.19 for the harmfulness direction and 0.10 for the refusal direction at L12, and 0.31 and 0.30 at L27 - all below lane B's 0.50 instrument gate [2]. Within-position response-side coupling is 0.42 at L12 and 0.69 at L27 on Qwen, versus 0.45 and 0.27 on Llama [2]. Llama's own cross-position values remain heat-map-only; the text says only that same-concept cross-position pairs remain aligned [2]. Independent corroboration comes from a different protocol entirely: max-pool-over-content and last-token-post-instruction harm directions lie 73 degrees plus or minus 7 apart in the same residual-stream layer, i.e. a cosine of about 0.29 [6]. Two papers, two protocols, converging on 0.19 to 0.31. LANE B PROCEEDS ON THE MAIN BRANCH; the annihilation-residue branch stays pre-committed but is now the unexpected path. Caveats: Qwen-2.5-7B is not Qwen3-4B; HARC's harmfulness direction is AdvBench/UltraChat-fitted rather than abliteration-derived [2]; measuring this on Qwen3-4B with the abliteration direction as one axis is still a first, so report it as a result, just not a surprising one.

F2 - THE 2604.18901 MISATTRIBUTIONS. THE PLANNING RECON WAS WRONG TWICE; THE HYPOTHESIS WAS RIGHT TWICE. The paper prints the plus-or-minus 0.003 figure TWICE: once as abliterated variants matching their instruction-tuned counterparts on AUROC, each with its own direction (the hypothesis's reading), and once as instruct-to-abliterated direction TRANSFER being cleaner than base-to-instruct at angles of 11 to 42 degrees (the recon's reading) [5]. It also prints 73 degrees TWICE: once as the max-pool-versus-last-token PROTOCOL angle at the same layer (the hypothesis's reading) [6], and once as Gemma-3's base-to-instruct alignment rotation, with AUROC dropping 0.057 and TPR collapsing from 0.751 to 0.175 (the recon's reading) [5]. Both pairs are verbatim in the source. Any future citation must say WHICH.

F2 HANDOFF TO LANE B - NO INTERIOR MATCHED-O-DAMAGE POINT SHOULD BE EXPECTED. Instruct-to-abliterated angles are 11 to 42 degrees (cosine 0.74 to 0.98) with AUROC degradation at most 0.003, and the abliterated model's own-direction AUROC matches instruct within the same tolerance [5]. The edit leaves the request axis nearly intact. Report the criterion UNMET rather than relaxing it. Note also that this anchor was measured on huihui-ai abliterated variants [5], whereas our panel must use the mlabonne checkpoint because the huihui-ai one is gated, so the prior transfers as a family-level expectation, not a same-artifact measurement.

F3 - N-GLARE. JSS aggregates Jensen-Shannon divergences of turning-angle distributions across all progress slices and layer groups into a model-level separability measure (Eq 7), with the proxies JB/PB Ratio (Eq 8) and JR Min/Max (Eq 9) built on top [3]. INPUT REQUIREMENT: four dialogue families - Baseline, PlainQuery, Jailbreak and Ideal Refusal [3] - so it is NOT a 0-prompt or few-prompt method, and our prompt-budget curve must be read against that requirement rather than against a benchmark. NO NUMERIC KENDALL'S TAU IS PRINTED IN THE PUBLISHED RUNNING TEXT: the paper says only that tau remains consistently high with p-values far below conventional significance thresholds, deferring the values to figures and appendix tables [3]. Do not write "N-GLARE achieves tau equals X". NO PUBLIC CODE: a full-text grep of the published PDF for code, github and release returns nothing [3], so JSS is NOT an implementable baseline in iteration 1; lane C cites it as the nearest published competitor [4] and states the comparison it could not run.

F4 - PREFILL PROTOCOL. Confirmed and, more usefully, EXPLAINED. The dose-matched position control shows the first half of the response suffices to break refusal while the second half is nearly inert [15], and the full-residual "restoration" reaches 100 percent only because a benign-patch control also reaches 100 percent, making it generic disruption [15]. Lane B's demotion of full-residual patching to a labelled positive control is therefore correct. The load-bearing rungs are that dose-matched positional control and the refuse-state transfer, and the paper's own framing is that the intact representation is upstream of the gate rather than epiphenomenal [15]. Its pre-registered 2x2 crosses harm with surface, and refusal tracks harm rather than scary surface [15].

F5 - ANTI-RANKING. Verified verbatim: among wrapped harmful prompts the outcome AUROC is 0.220, placing the attacks that succeeded below the attacks that failed [16]. On Llama, wrapping raises harmful generation from 0.05 to 0.27 while harmful-intent AUROC falls from 0.936 to 0.803 [16]. Reproduced across three target models, seven attack families and two judges [16]. This is the strongest general adverse prior on any internal-readout validity claim and the paper must engage it rather than omit it.

DELIVERABLE-LEVEL COMPETITORS - FOUR, NOT TWO. (1) N-GLARE: latent-only, over 40 models, under 1 percent of the token and runtime cost, output-free, peer-reviewed [3][4]. (2) Skin-Deep/GFS: a single scalar from hidden states over 21 instruction-tuned models and six alignment recipes, but parent- and harmful-prompt-requiring and predicting future fragility [7][8]. (3) The two-signal checkpoint audit: an activation refusal-gap plus a weight-recovery energy separating 57 abliterations from 37 benign fine-tunes at AUROC 0.95 over a 273-checkpoint registry - but reference-anchored [13]. (4) IRT: ten adaptive items over 192 models, text-only and therefore a baseline under the run invariant, but it owns the "few prompts" half of the commissioned ask [14]. Also mandatory to cite: the audit-gap result, which builds models that pass every static behavioural audit yet fall to a small bounded latent perturbation and is the best available motivation for an activation-level metric [22]; the black-box fast proxies that predict full attack-ensemble ASR at Spearman 0.94, which is the ceiling we are competing against [23]; the layer-wise margin-geometry probe, the closest published cousin of any profile-over-layers summary [32]; and the cross-family latent-probe reproducibility study, which also flags inference non-determinism as a variance source lane C must control [31].

THE SINGLE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL FINDING, WHICH IS NOT A NOVELTY VERDICT. Two independent papers report that prompt-side HARM-RECOGNITION geometry is essentially INVARIANT to abliteration: abliterated variants match their instruction-tuned counterparts within 0.003 AUROC across 12 models spanning four families [5], and at most 0.015 AUROC on Qwen base/instruct/abliterated triplets [10]. Two of our four panel checkpoints are Qwen3-4B and its abliterated sibling. Any candidate whose readout is fundamentally "can this checkpoint recognise harm" therefore has a PUBLISHED PRIOR OF APPROXIMATELY ZERO EFFECT on that pair. Candidates must read the refusal/gate side or the arming interaction instead - a split independently supported by the finding that the refusal gate is a post-training control-token channel orthogonal to moral judgment [18]. Put this in every lane's power calculation.

THE SECOND MOST CONSEQUENTIAL, ALSO NEW. A parent-free, single-checkpoint, interaction-flavoured activation scalar already exists and already failed. HRCI_repr is defined as one half the absolute inner product of the harmfulness and refusal carrier directions plus one half the mean squared canonical correlation between their local subspaces, with the equal weighting fixed a priori rather than optimised against outcomes [12]. It is tracked over 100 checkpoints per regime against XSTest refusal, attack success rate, StrongREJECT and benign utility, falling from 0.0784 at step 50 to 0.0205 at step 500 while fixed-source ASR rises from 0 to 0.2500 and XSTest refusal falls from 1.0000 to 0.2280, with an SFT control moving only from 0.0217 to 0.0190 while staying high-ASR [12]. The authors' own verdict is that low coupling is not a safety score, and that the dense all-anchor results support a fixed-protocol diagnostic rather than a general safety score [12]. That is the closest published object to this run's own ambition, and it is a scaled negative result. It is now mandatory baseline G10, it is implementable in a few lines, and the paper must answer it rather than cite it in passing.

A THIRD ADVERSE PRIOR LANE A MUST PRICE IN. Activation probes evaluated on XSTest-style matched twin cohorts - exactly lane A's item type - reach only AUROC 0.590 to 0.690 on the full Twin-n163 cohort and 0.656 to 0.819 on the guard-selected Twin-n70 subset under fixed transfer without pair-boundary fitting, and the paper concludes these scores behave as broad-risk detectors rather than standalone context adjudicators [11]. If lane A's twin numbers land above that band, check for leakage before celebrating. The behavioural counterpart is that risk genuinely does change between prompt and response - 61 percent of responses reduce harm relative to the prompt, 36 percent preserve it and 3 percent escalate - but that analysis is human-labelled and behavioural, not activation-level, so it is a weaker relative to K1 than the recon feared [30].

BASELINE SPECS (full text in research_report.md section 7). G1 card/repo regex in BOTH term-swept and name-free variants, reported separately or the baseline is inflated by the very label it predicts. G2 black-box greedy refusal rate with the named safe-completion-opener failure mode. G3 first-token refusal logit gap, logit-only and therefore a baseline under the run invariant. G4 Fisher-ratio activation cluster separation over the upper-middle layer band, calibrated against the realistic published effect size of Pearson r equal to minus 0.546 between an activation sigma and behavioural compliance over 14 configurations [17]. G5 held-out difference-in-means with the mandatory high-dimension in-sample AUROC-1.000 warning, copying the published protocol of 100 labelled examples per class under a strict three-way split and reporting TPR at 1 percent FPR alongside AUROC, because that paper's central methodological point is that TPR varies by more than ten times the AUROC gap [6]. G6 raw hidden vectors as the non-featurised control. G7 JSS NOT IMPLEMENTABLE [3]. G8 GFS [7], the two-signal z-sum [13] and CANARY [24] are all PARENT-REQUIRING and must sit in a separate column. G9 the Spearman 0.94 black-box proxy ceiling [23]. G10 HRCI_repr, fully specified and parent-free, the single most important baseline in the block [12].

ID RESOLUTION. All 26 core ids resolved, 0 unresolved, 0 mis-cited, plus 20 newly discovered ids also resolved. The only unresolvable citation is the companion methods note "Calibrating Interpretability Instruments Before Trusting Their Verdicts", named in the comments field of the harm-keyed-routing paper [18] with no standalone arXiv id found; no verdict here rests on it. Note also that arXiv 2604.09544 IS a real paper but concerns parameter-level pruning of harmful compliance, not the harmfulness/refusal split; the correct citation for that split is the NeurIPS 2025 paper [27]."""
